# 📋 Phylogenle Excluded & Deferred Species Review Log

**Last Updated:** 2026-10-23 20:05:42
**Total Active Species in Game:** 29748
**Total Candidates Promoted to Active Roster:** 3683
**Currently Excluded:** 0

### ✅ Full Ingestion Status

Per user directive to include all excluded species and maximize the playable species pool without artificial Metazoa/Animalia barriers, **all previously excluded and deferred candidates have been promoted and integrated directly into the active game catalog**.

This includes non-animal organisms (Plants, Algae, Fungi, Bacteria, Protists), sister species, domestic/subspecies forms, and microscopic taxa.
